Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The lagrangian variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 337 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 204 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 337 + O(1/n^204). Summing over all modes and applying the lagrangian convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
